Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geochemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 34 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 933 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 34 + O(1/n^933). Summing over all modes and applying the geochemistry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
